% 用法：\zh{中文} / \sym{→} / \emo{🦙}
% 注意：必须**带参数**。写成 \def\zh{{\zhfont}} 是错的——字体赋值只在小群里有效，
% 那个小群随 \zh 立刻结束，于是中文仍然用拉丁字体排，报 Missing character。
\def\zh#1{{\zhfont #1}}
\def\sym#1{{\symfont #1}}
\def\emo#1{{\emofont #1}}
% 含汉字的行内代码：listings 的 \lstinline 遇到汉字必炸（`\lst@arg ->判`

\zh{我\zhglue 们\zhglue 的\zhglue 情\zhglue 况\zhglue 是}''\zh{几\zhglue 亿\zhglue 个\zhglue 参\zhglue 数\zhglue （\zhglue 输\zhglue 入\zhglue 多\zhglue ）}\sym{→} \zh{一\zhglue 个\zhglue 标\zhglue 量} loss\zh{（\zhglue 输\zhglue 出\zhglue 少\zhglue ）}``\zh{，} \zh{所\zhglue 以}\textbf{\zh{反\zhglue 向\zhglue 模\zhglue 式\zhglue （\zhglue 就\zhglue 是\zhglue 我\zhglue 们\zhglue 说\zhglue 的\zhglue 反\zhglue 向\zhglue 传\zhglue 播\zhglue ）}}\zh{是\zhglue 天\zhglue 选\zhglue ：}\textbf{1 \zh{次\zhglue 前\zhglue 向} + 1 \zh{次\zhglue 反\zhglue 向} = \zh{全\zhglue 部\zhglue 梯\zhglue 度}}\zh{。}

\textbf{\zh{梯\zhglue 度\zhglue 累\zhglue 加\zhglue 的\zhglue 工\zhglue 程\zhglue 含\zhglue 义}}\zh{：\zhglue 反\zhglue 向\zhglue 时\zhglue 每\zhglue 个\zhglue 节\zhglue 点\zhglue 把\zhglue 上\zhglue 游\zhglue 梯\zhglue 度}\textbf{\zh{相\zhglue 加}}\zh{。\zhglue 这\zhglue 解\zhglue 释\zhglue 了\zhglue 两\zhglue 个\zhglue 常\zhglue 见\zhglue 现\zhglue 象\zhglue ：} \sym{①}\zh{同\zhglue 一\zhglue 个\zhglue 张\zhglue 量\zhglue 被\zhglue 用\zhglue 了\zhglue 两\zhglue 次\zhglue （\zhglue 如\zhglue 权\zhglue 重\zhglue 共\zhglue 享\zhglue ）\zhglue ，\zhglue 梯\zhglue 度\zhglue 会\zhglue 累\zhglue 加\zhglue ；} \sym{②}\zh{既\zhglue 然\zhglue 梯\zhglue 度\zhglue 是}''\zh{累\zhglue 加}''\zh{的\zhglue ，\zhglue 就\zhglue 能}\textbf{\zh{梯\zhglue 度\zhglue 累\zhglue 积}}\zh{（}gradient accumulation\zh{）}------\zh{多\zhglue 个\zhglue 小\zhglue 批\zhglue 次\zhglue 的\zhglue 梯\zhglue 度\zhglue 加\zhglue 起\zhglue 来\zhglue 等\zhglue 价\zhglue 于\zhglue 大\zhglue 批\zhglue 次\zhglue ，} \zh{这\zhglue 是\zhglue 后\zhglue 来\zhglue 显\zhglue 存\zhglue 不\zhglue 够\zhglue 时\zhglue 的\zhglue 核\zhglue 心\zhglue 手\zhglue 段\zhglue （\zhglue 课\zhglue 题}04 \zh{会\zhglue 用\zhglue 到\zhglue ）\zhglue 。}

\textbf{\zh{在\zhglue 代\zhglue 码\zhglue 里\zhglue 长\zhglue 什\zhglue 么\zhglue 样}}\zh{：} - \passthrough{\codezh{{\zh{手}}{\zh{写}}/}} \zh{里\zhglue 会\zhglue 有\zhglue 一\zhglue 个} \passthrough{\lstinline!Value!} \zh{类\zhglue （\zhglue 或\zhglue 等\zhglue 价\zhglue 的\zhglue 张\zhglue 量\zhglue 自\zhglue 动\zhglue 微\zhglue 分\zhglue ）\zhglue ，\zhglue 核\zhglue 心\zhglue 是} \passthrough{\lstinline!.backward()!}\zh{：} \zh{按}\textbf{\zh{拓\zhglue 扑\zhglue 逆\zhglue 序}}\zh{遍\zhglue 历\zhglue ，\zhglue 每\zhglue 个\zhglue 节\zhglue 点\zhglue 用\zhglue 局\zhglue 部\zhglue 导\zhglue 数\zhglue 乘\zhglue 上\zhglue 游\zhglue 梯\zhglue 度\zhglue 累\zhglue 加\zhglue 到\zhglue 父\zhglue 节\zhglue 点\zhglue 。} - \zh{上\zhglue 游\zhglue 对\zhglue 照\zhglue ：}\passthrough{\lstinline!nanochat/optim.py!} \zh{里\zhglue 参\zhglue 数\zhglue 更\zhglue 新\zhglue 前\zhglue 的} \passthrough{\lstinline!p.grad!} \zh{就\zhglue 是\zhglue 这\zhglue 一\zhglue 机\zhglue 制\zhglue 的\zhglue 产\zhglue 物\zhglue ；}CS336 A1 \zh{的} \passthrough{\lstinline!run\_gradient\_clipping!} \zh{则\zhglue 是}\textbf{\zh{在\zhglue 使\zhglue 用}}\zh{梯\zhglue 度\zhglue （\zhglue 先\zhglue 要\zhglue 能\zhglue 算\zhglue 出\zhglue 来\zhglue ）\zhglue 。}

\subsection{\texorpdfstring{\zh{二\zhglue 、\zhglue 信\zhglue 息\zhglue 量\zhglue 、\zhglue 交\zhglue 叉\zhglue 熵\zhglue 、\zhglue 困\zhglue 惑\zhglue 度\zhglue 、}bpb\zh{（}\textbf{\zh{跨\zhglue 分\zhglue 词\zhglue 器\zhglue 可\zhglue 比\zhglue 性}}\zh{的\zhglue 根\zhglue 源\zhglue ）}}{\zh{二\zhglue 、\zhglue 信\zhglue 息\zhglue 量\zhglue 、\zhglue 交\zhglue 叉\zhglue 熵\zhglue 、\zhglue 困\zhglue 惑\zhglue 度\zhglue 、}bpb\zh{（\zhglue 跨\zhglue 分\zhglue 词\zhglue 器\zhglue 可\zhglue 比\zhglue 性\zhglue 的\zhglue 根\zhglue 源\zhglue ）}}}\label{ux4e8cux4fe1ux606fux91cfux4ea4ux53c9ux71b5ux56f0ux60d1ux5ea6bpbux8de8ux5206ux8bcdux5668ux53efux6bd4ux6027ux7684ux6839ux6e90}

\textbf{\zh{信\zhglue 息\zhglue 量}}\zh{：\zhglue 一\zhglue 个\zhglue 概\zhglue 率\zhglue 为} \(p\) \zh{的\zhglue 事\zhglue 件\zhglue 携\zhglue 带\zhglue 的\zhglue 信\zhglue 息\zhglue 是} \(-\log p\)\zh{（}nats\zh{，\zhglue 底\zhglue 为} \(e\)\zh{；\zhglue 若\zhglue 底\zhglue 为} 2 \zh{则\zhglue 单\zhglue 位\zhglue 是} bit\zh{）\zhglue 。}

\textbf{\zh{熵}}\zh{：\zhglue 随\zhglue 机\zhglue 变\zhglue 量\zhglue 的\zhglue 平\zhglue 均\zhglue 信\zhglue 息\zhglue 量} \(H(p) = -\sum_x p(x)\log p(x)\)\zh{。} \zh{直\zhglue 觉\zhglue ：}\textbf{``\zh{平\zhglue 均\zhglue 每\zhglue 个\zhglue 符\zhglue 号\zhglue 有\zhglue 多\zhglue 难\zhglue 猜}''}\zh{。}

\textbf{\zh{交\zhglue 叉\zhglue 熵}}\zh{：\zhglue 用\zhglue 模\zhglue 型\zhglue 分\zhglue 布} \(q\) \zh{去\zhglue 编\zhglue 码\zhglue 真\zhglue 实\zhglue 分\zhglue 布} \(p\) \zh{的\zhglue 代\zhglue 价} \[H(p,q) = -\sum_x p(x)\log q(x)\] \zh{语\zhglue 言\zhglue 模\zhglue 型\zhglue 里} \(p\) \zh{是}''\zh{真\zhglue 实\zhglue 下\zhglue 一\zhglue 个} token''\zh{，}\(q\) \zh{是\zhglue 模\zhglue 型\zhglue 输\zhglue 出} ------ \textbf{\zh{这\zhglue 正\zhglue 好\zhglue 就\zhglue 是\zhglue 训\zhglue 练} loss}\zh{。}

\textbf{\zh{困\zhglue 惑\zhglue 度}}\zh{：}\(\mathrm{PPL} = \exp(H(p,q))\)\zh{（}nats \zh{为\zhglue 底\zhglue 时\zhglue ）\zhglue 。} \zh{直\zhglue 觉\zhglue 最\zhglue 有\zhglue 用\zhglue 的\zhglue 一\zhglue 句\zhglue 话\zhglue ：}\textbf{``\zh{模\zhglue 型\zhglue 平\zhglue 均\zhglue 在\zhglue 多\zhglue 少\zhglue 个\zhglue 等\zhglue 概\zhglue 率\zhglue 选\zhglue 项\zhglue 里\zhglue 犯\zhglue 迷\zhglue 糊\zhglue 。}''} PPL=10 \zh{相\zhglue 当\zhglue 于}''\zh{平\zhglue 均} 10 \zh{选} 1''\zh{。}

\textbf{bpb\zh{（}bits per byte\zh{）}}\zh{：} \[\mathrm{bpb} = \frac{H(p,q)\ \text{[nats]}}{\ln 2 \times \overline{\#\text{bytes per token}}}\] \zh{分\zhglue 母\zhglue 是}''\zh{平\zhglue 均\zhglue 每\zhglue 个} token \zh{对\zhglue 应\zhglue 多\zhglue 少\zhglue 字\zhglue 节}''\zh{。}

\begin{quote}
\textbf{\zh{为\zhglue 什\zhglue 么\zhglue 必\zhglue 须\zhglue 用} bpb \zh{跨\zhglue 模\zhglue 型\zhglue 比\zhglue 较}}\zh{：\zhglue 交\zhglue 叉\zhglue 熵\zhglue 的\zhglue 单\zhglue 位\zhglue 是}''\zh{每} token''\zh{，\zhglue 而} token \zh{的\zhglue 大\zhglue 小\zhglue 随\zhglue 分\zhglue 词\zhglue 器\zhglue 变\zhglue 化}------ \zh{词\zhglue 表\zhglue 越\zhglue 大} token \zh{越}''\zh{大}''\zh{，\zhglue 同\zhglue 样\zhglue 文\zhglue 本\zhglue 的} token \zh{数\zhglue 越\zhglue 少\zhglue ，\zhglue 单} token \zh{交\zhglue 叉\zhglue 熵\zhglue 天\zhglue 然\zhglue 越\zhglue 高\zhglue 。} \textbf{\zh{除\zhglue 以}''\zh{每} token \zh{字\zhglue 节\zhglue 数}''\zh{就\zhglue 把\zhglue 记\zhglue 账\zhglue 单\zhglue 位\zhglue 统\zhglue 一\zhglue 到}''\zh{每\zhglue 个\zhglue 字\zhglue 节}''\zh{上\zhglue ，\zhglue 于\zhglue 是\zhglue 与\zhglue 分\zhglue 词\zhglue 器\zhglue 无\zhglue 关\zhglue 。}} \zh{这\zhglue 也\zhglue 解\zhglue 释\zhglue 了} M2 \zh{讲\zhglue 义\zhglue 的\zhglue 结\zhglue 论\zhglue ：\zhglue 换\zhglue 词\zhglue 表\zhglue 后} loss \zh{不\zhglue 可\zhglue 比\zhglue ，}bpb \zh{可\zhglue 比\zhglue 。}
\end{quote}

\textbf{\zh{在\zhglue 代\zhglue 码\zhglue 里\zhglue 长\zhglue 什\zhglue 么\zhglue 样}}\zh{：}\passthrough{\lstinline!nanochat/loss\_eval.py!} \zh{专\zhglue 门\zhglue 做} bpb\zh{（\zhglue 而\zhglue 不\zhglue 是} loss\zh{）\zhglue 的\zhglue 评\zhglue 估\zhglue ；} \passthrough{\lstinline!nanochat/core\_eval.py!} \zh{则\zhglue 是\zhglue 另\zhglue 一\zhglue 类\zhglue 指\zhglue 标\zhglue （}CORE\zh{，\zhglue 由} 22 \zh{个\zhglue 数\zhglue 据\zhglue 集\zhglue 的\zhglue 准\zhglue 确\zhglue 率\zhglue 合\zhglue 成\zhglue ）\zhglue 。} \textbf{\zh{你\zhglue 在\zhglue 课\zhglue 题}01 \zh{的\zhglue 压\zhglue 缩\zhglue 率\zhglue 表\zhglue ，\zhglue 其\zhglue 实\zhglue 是\zhglue 在\zhglue 预\zhglue 演} bpb \zh{的\zhglue 分\zhglue 母}}------\zh{先\zhglue 感\zhglue 受}''\zh{每} token \zh{多\zhglue 少\zhglue 字\zhglue 节}''\zh{。}

\subsection{\texorpdfstring{\zh{三\zhglue 、\zhglue 算\zhglue 力\zhglue 与\zhglue 显\zhglue 存\zhglue 记\zhglue 账\zhglue （}\textbf{\zh{估\zhglue 数\zhglue 量\zhglue 级}}\zh{是\zhglue 核\zhglue 心\zhglue 能\zhglue 力\zhglue ）}}{\zh{三\zhglue 、\zhglue 算\zhglue 力\zhglue 与\zhglue 显\zhglue 存\zhglue 记\zhglue 账\zhglue （\zhglue 估\zhglue 数\zhglue 量\zhglue 级\zhglue 是\zhglue 核\zhglue 心\zhglue 能\zhglue 力\zhglue ）}}}\label{ux4e09ux7b97ux529bux4e0eux663eux5b58ux8bb0ux8d26ux4f30ux6570ux91cfux7ea7ux662fux6838ux5fc3ux80fdux529b}

\textbf{\zh{参\zhglue 数\zhglue 量}}\zh{（}Transformer \zh{主\zhglue 项\zhglue ，\zhglue 细\zhglue 节\zhglue 见} M3\zh{）\zhglue ：} - \zh{每\zhglue 层\zhglue 注\zhglue 意\zhglue 力} \(4d^2\)\zh{（}Q/K/V/O \zh{四\zhglue 个} \(d\times d\)\zh{）\zhglue ，}MLP \zh{约} \(8d^2\)\zh{（}SwiGLU \zh{的\zhglue 等\zhglue 价\zhglue 形\zhglue 式\zhglue ）}\sym{→} \textbf{\zh{每\zhglue 层} \sym{≈} \(12d^2\)} - \zh{词\zhglue 嵌\zhglue 入} \(V\cdot d\)\zh{（\zhglue 权\zhglue 重\zhglue 共\zhglue 享\zhglue 时} 1 \zh{份\zhglue ）}

\textbf{\zh{算\zhglue 力\zhglue （\zhglue 训\zhglue 练\zhglue ）}}\zh{：\zhglue 业\zhglue 界\zhglue 通\zhglue 用\zhglue 估\zhglue 算} \[\text{\zh{总} FLOPs} \approx 6 \times N \times D\] \zh{其\zhglue 中} \(N\) \zh{是}\textbf{\zh{非\zhglue 嵌\zhglue 入\zhglue 参\zhglue 数\zhglue 量}}\zh{，}\(D\) \zh{是\zhglue 训\zhglue 练} token \zh{数\zhglue 。\zhglue 拆\zhglue 解\zhglue ：\zhglue 每\zhglue 个\zhglue 参\zhglue 数\zhglue 在\zhglue 前\zhglue 向\zhglue 贡\zhglue 献} 2 FLOPs\zh{（\zhglue 乘}+\zh{加\zhglue ）\zhglue ，} \zh{反\zhglue 向\zhglue 是\zhglue 前\zhglue 向\zhglue 的} 2 \zh{倍} \sym{→} \zh{合\zhglue 计} 6\zh{。\zhglue 注\zhglue 意\zhglue 力\zhglue 部\zhglue 分\zhglue 另\zhglue 有} \(12 L h q t\) \zh{项\zhglue （\zhglue 序\zhglue 列\zhglue 长\zhglue 度\zhglue 相\zhglue 关\zhglue ，\zhglue 短\zhglue 序\zhglue 列\zhglue 时\zhglue 可\zhglue 忽\zhglue 略\zhglue ）\zhglue 。} \textgreater{} Karpathy \zh{在} nanochat \zh{文\zhglue 档\zhglue 里\zhglue 给\zhglue 的\zhglue 就\zhglue 是\zhglue 这\zhglue 个\zhglue 公\zhglue 式\zhglue ，\zhglue 并\zhglue 特\zhglue 别\zhglue 注\zhglue 明}''\zh{与} Chinchilla \zh{略\zhglue 有} 1\% \zh{差\zhglue 异\zhglue ，\zhglue 因\zhglue 为\zhglue 不\zhglue 计\zhglue 嵌\zhglue 入\zhglue 与} softmax''\zh{。}

\textbf{\zh{显\zhglue 存\zhglue （\zhglue 训\zhglue 练\zhglue ）}}\zh{：\zhglue 粗\zhglue 算\zhglue 每\zhglue 参\zhglue 数} \textbar{} \zh{项} \textbar{} \zh{字\zhglue 节\zhglue 数} \textbar{} \textbar---\textbar---\textbar{} \textbar{} \zh{参\zhglue 数\zhglue （}bf16\zh{）} \textbar{} 2 \textbar{} \textbar{} \zh{梯\zhglue 度\zhglue （}bf16\zh{）} \textbar{} 2 \textbar{} \textbar{} Adam \zh{一\zhglue 阶\zhglue 动\zhglue 量\zhglue （}fp32\zh{）} \textbar{} 4 \textbar{} \textbar{} Adam \zh{二\zhglue 阶\zhglue 动\zhglue 量\zhglue （}fp32\zh{）} \textbar{} 4 \textbar{} \textbar{} \textbf{\zh{小\zhglue 计\zhglue （\zhglue 不\zhglue 含\zhglue 激\zhglue 活\zhglue 、\zhglue 不\zhglue 含} fp32 \zh{主\zhglue 权\zhglue 重\zhglue ）}} \textbar{} \textbf{\sym{≈} 12--16} \textbar{} \textbar{} \zh{激\zhglue 活\zhglue （\zhglue 随} batch \sym{×} \zh{序\zhglue 列\zhglue 长\zhglue 度\zhglue 增\zhglue 长\zhglue ，\zhglue 可\zhglue 用} checkpointing \zh{换\zhglue ）} \textbar{} \zh{视\zhglue 配\zhglue 置} \textbar{}

\textbf{\zh{练\zhglue 习\zhglue 式\zhglue 直\zhglue 觉}}\zh{：}0.1B \zh{参\zhglue 数} \sym{×} 16 \zh{字\zhglue 节} \sym{≈} 1.6GB\zh{（\zhglue 优\zhglue 化\zhglue 器}+\zh{梯\zhglue 度}+\zh{权\zhglue 重\zhglue ）\zhglue ；} \zh{剩\zhglue 下\zhglue 的\zhglue 全\zhglue 是\zhglue 激\zhglue 活\zhglue 与\zhglue 临\zhglue 时\zhglue 张\zhglue 量} ------ \textbf{\zh{这\zhglue 就\zhglue 是\zhglue 为\zhglue 什\zhglue 么} 16GB \zh{的} T4 \zh{训} 0.1B \zh{要\zhglue 精\zhglue 打\zhglue 细\zhglue 算}}\zh{（\zhglue 课\zhglue 题}04 \zh{的\zhglue 真\zhglue 实\zhglue 约\zhglue 束\zhglue ）\zhglue 。}

\textbf{\zh{在\zhglue 代\zhglue 码\zhglue 里\zhglue 长\zhglue 什\zhglue 么\zhglue 样}}\zh{：}\passthrough{\lstinline!nanochat/gpt.py!} \zh{里\zhglue 有} \passthrough{\lstinline!estimate\_flops()!} \zh{与} \passthrough{\lstinline!num\_scaling\_params()!} \zh{两\zhglue 个\zhglue 方\zhglue 法\zhglue ，} \zh{正\zhglue 是\zhglue 上\zhglue 面\zhglue 两\zhglue 段\zhglue 公\zhglue 式\zhglue 的\zhglue 代\zhglue 码\zhglue 化\zhglue 。}\textbf{\zh{课\zhglue 题}03 \zh{的} scaling law \zh{就\zhglue 是\zhglue 靠\zhglue 这\zhglue 两\zhglue 个\zhglue 函\zhglue 数\zhglue 把}''\zh{跑\zhglue 多\zhglue 大}''\zh{换\zhglue 算\zhglue 成}''\zh{花\zhglue 多\zhglue 少\zhglue 算\zhglue 力}''\zh{的\zhglue 。}}

\subsection{\zh{四\zhglue 、\zhglue 数\zhglue 值\zhglue 稳\zhglue 定\zhglue 性\zhglue ：}softmax \zh{与} logsumexp}\label{ux56dbux6570ux503cux7a33ux5b9aux6027softmax-ux4e0e-logsumexp}

\textbf{\zh{问\zhglue 题}}\zh{：}\(\mathrm{softmax}(x)_i = e^{x_i}/\sum_j e^{x_j}\)\zh{。\zhglue 若\zhglue 某\zhglue 个} \(x_i = 1000\)\zh{，}\(e^{1000}\) \zh{直\zhglue 接\zhglue 溢\zhglue 出\zhglue 成} \passthrough{\lstinline!inf!}\zh{。}

\textbf{\zh{解\zhglue 法}}\zh{：\zhglue 分\zhglue 子\zhglue 分\zhglue 母\zhglue 同\zhglue 乘} \(e^{-m}\)\zh{（}\(m=\max_j x_j\)\zh{）\zhglue ：} \[\mathrm{softmax}(x)_i = \frac{e^{x_i-m}}{\sum_j e^{x_j-m}}\] \zh{数\zhglue 值\zhglue 上\zhglue 完\zhglue 全\zhglue 等\zhglue 价\zhglue ，\zhglue 但\zhglue 最\zhglue 大\zhglue 指\zhglue 数\zhglue 变\zhglue 成} \(e^0=1\)\zh{，\zhglue 永\zhglue 不\zhglue 溢\zhglue 出\zhglue 。}\textbf{``\zh{减\zhglue 最\zhglue 大\zhglue 值}''\zh{不\zhglue 是\zhglue 技\zhglue 巧\zhglue ，\zhglue 是\zhglue 恒\zhglue 等\zhglue 变\zhglue 形\zhglue 。}}

\textbf{logsumexp \zh{技\zhglue 巧}}\zh{：}\(\log\sum_j e^{x_j} = m + \log\sum_j e^{x_j - m}\)\zh{，} \zh{这\zhglue 是\zhglue 所\zhglue 有}''\zh{用} log \zh{概\zhglue 率\zhglue 做\zhglue 加\zhglue 法}''\zh{的\zhglue 算\zhglue 法\zhglue （\zhglue 交\zhglue 叉\zhglue 熵\zhglue 、}CTC\zh{、}HMM\zh{、}flash attention \zh{的} online softmax\zh{）\zhglue 的\zhglue 共\zhglue 同\zhglue 基\zhglue 础\zhglue 。}

\textbf{\zh{为\zhglue 什\zhglue 么\zhglue 这\zhglue 很\zhglue 重\zhglue 要}}\zh{：\zhglue 课\zhglue 题}05 \zh{的} \textbf{online softmax} \zh{就\zhglue 是}''\zh{分\zhglue 块\zhglue 计\zhglue 算\zhglue 时\zhglue 如\zhglue 何\zhglue 边\zhglue 算\zhglue 边\zhglue 保\zhglue 持\zhglue 数\zhglue 值\zhglue 稳\zhglue 定}''\zh{，} \zh{其\zhglue 思\zhglue 想\zhglue 内\zhglue 核\zhglue 与\zhglue 这\zhglue 里\zhglue 的\zhglue 减\zhglue 最\zhglue 大\zhglue 值\zhglue 完\zhglue 全\zhglue 一\zhglue 致}------\textbf{\zh{你\zhglue 在} M0 \zh{学\zhglue 的\zhglue 东\zhglue 西\zhglue ，\zhglue 会\zhglue 在} Triton kernel \zh{里\zhglue 原\zhglue 样\zhglue 再\zhglue 出\zhglue 现\zhglue 一\zhglue 次\zhglue 。}}

# (*@{\sym{①}}@*) (*@{\zh{手}}@*)(*@{\zh{算}}@*) bpb (*@{\zh{的}}@*)(*@{\zh{换}}@*)(*@{\zh{算}}@*)(*@{\zh{（}}@*)(*@{\zh{先}}@*)(*@{\zh{自}}@*)(*@{\zh{己}}@*)(*@{\zh{推}}@*)(*@{\zh{，}}@*)(*@{\zh{再}}@*)(*@{\zh{看}}@*)(*@{\zh{输}}@*)(*@{\zh{出}}@*)(*@{\zh{对}}@*)(*@{\zh{不}}@*)(*@{\zh{对}}@*)(*@{\zh{）}}@*)
(*@{\zh{环}}@*)(*@{\zh{境}}@*)/.venv/bin/python - <<'PY'
import math
# (*@{\zh{假}}@*)(*@{\zh{设}}@*)(*@{\zh{某}}@*)(*@{\zh{模}}@*)(*@{\zh{型}}@*)(*@{\zh{：}}@*)(*@{\zh{每}}@*) token (*@{\zh{交}}@*)(*@{\zh{叉}}@*)(*@{\zh{熵}}@*) 2.0 nats(*@{\zh{，}}@*)(*@{\zh{平}}@*)(*@{\zh{均}}@*)(*@{\zh{每}}@*)(*@{\zh{个}}@*) token (*@{\zh{对}}@*)(*@{\zh{应}}@*) 4 (*@{\zh{个}}@*)(*@{\zh{字}}@*)(*@{\zh{节}}@*)
loss_nats, bytes_per_token = 2.0, 4.0

bpb = loss_nats / math.log(2) / bytes_per_token

print("(*@{\sym{→}}@*) (*@{\zh{再}}@*)(*@{\zh{推}}@*)(*@{\zh{一}}@*)(*@{\zh{遍}}@*)(*@{\zh{：}}@*)(*@{\zh{若}}@*)(*@{\zh{词}}@*)(*@{\zh{表}}@*)(*@{\zh{翻}}@*)(*@{\zh{倍}}@*)(*@{\zh{使}}@*) bytes/token (*@{\zh{变}}@*)(*@{\zh{成}}@*) 6(*@{\zh{，}}@*)bpb (*@{\zh{会}}@*)(*@{\zh{怎}}@*)(*@{\zh{么}}@*)(*@{\zh{变}}@*)(*@{\zh{？}}@*)loss (*@{\zh{呢}}@*)(*@{\zh{？}}@*)")
PY

# (*@{\sym{②}}@*) (*@{\zh{验}}@*)(*@{\zh{证}}@*)"(*@{\zh{减}}@*)(*@{\zh{最}}@*)(*@{\zh{大}}@*)(*@{\zh{值}}@*)"(*@{\zh{是}}@*)(*@{\zh{恒}}@*)(*@{\zh{等}}@*)(*@{\zh{变}}@*)(*@{\zh{形}}@*)(*@{\zh{（}}@*)(*@{\zh{含}}@*)(*@{\zh{极}}@*)(*@{\zh{端}}@*)(*@{\zh{值}}@*)(*@{\zh{）}}@*)
(*@{\zh{环}}@*)(*@{\zh{境}}@*)/.venv/bin/python - <<'PY'
import numpy as np
x = np.array([0.0, 1000.0, 1000.0])
naive = np.exp(x) / np.exp(x).sum()      # (*@{\zh{溢}}@*)(*@{\zh{出}}@*)
stable = np.exp(x - x.max()) / np.exp(x - x.max()).sum()
print("naive :", naive, " (*@{\sym{←}}@*) (*@{\zh{溢}}@*)(*@{\zh{出}}@*)(*@{\zh{成}}@*) nan")
print("stable:", stable, " (*@{\sym{←}}@*) (*@{\zh{正}}@*)(*@{\zh{确}}@*)")
PY

  \zh{为\zhglue 什\zhglue 么\zhglue 说}''\zh{并\zhglue 行\zhglue 性}''\zh{是} Transformer \zh{取\zhglue 胜\zhglue 的\zhglue 主\zhglue 因\zhglue ，\zhglue 而\zhglue 不\zhglue 是}''\zh{注\zhglue 意\zhglue 力\zhglue 更\zhglue 聪\zhglue 明}''\zh{？}
\end{enumerate}

\subsection{\zh{一\zhglue 、}n-gram\zh{：\zhglue 用\zhglue 计\zhglue 数\zhglue 做\zhglue 语\zhglue 言\zhglue 模\zhglue 型}}\label{ux4e00n-gramux7528ux8ba1ux6570ux505aux8bedux8a00ux6a21ux578b}

\textbf{\zh{链\zhglue 式\zhglue 法\zhglue 则}}\zh{：}\(P(w_1\dots w_T) = \prod_t P(w_t \mid w_{<t})\)\zh{。} \textbf{n-gram \zh{的\zhglue 近\zhglue 似}}\zh{：\zhglue 只\zhglue 依\zhglue 赖\zhglue 前} \(n-1\) \zh{个\zhglue 词\zhglue ，}\(P(w_t\mid w_{t-n+1}\dots w_{t-1})\)\zh{，\zhglue 用}\textbf{\zh{语\zhglue 料\zhglue 计\zhglue 数}}\zh{估\zhglue 计\zhglue ：} \[P(w_t\mid w_{t-1}) = \frac{c(w_{t-1}w_t)}{c(w_{t-1})}\]

\textbf{\zh{两\zhglue 个\zhglue 死\zhglue 穴}}\zh{（\zhglue 这\zhglue 就\zhglue 是}''\zh{为\zhglue 什\zhglue 么\zhglue 历\zhglue 史\zhglue 必\zhglue 然\zhglue 翻\zhglue 篇}''\zh{）\zhglue ：} \textbar{} \zh{死\zhglue 穴} \textbar{} \zh{具\zhglue 体\zhglue 表\zhglue 现} \textbar{} \textbar---\textbar---\textbar{} \textbar{} \textbf{\zh{稀\zhglue 疏}} \textbar{} \zh{没\zhglue 见\zhglue 过\zhglue 的\zhglue 组\zhglue 合\zhglue 概\zhglue 率\zhglue 为} 0 \sym{→} \zh{平\zhglue 滑\zhglue （}add-k / \zh{回\zhglue 退} / Kneser-Ney\zh{）\zhglue 只\zhglue 是\zhglue 打\zhglue 补\zhglue 丁} \textbar{} \textbar{} \textbf{\zh{上\zhglue 下\zhglue 文\zhglue 太\zhglue 短}} \textbar{} \(n=5\) \zh{时\zhglue 也\zhglue 只\zhglue 能\zhglue 看} 4 \zh{个\zhglue 词\zhglue ；\zhglue 而}''\zh{这\zhglue 句\zhglue 话\zhglue 的\zhglue 主\zhglue 语\zhglue 是\zhglue 谁}''\zh{可\zhglue 能\zhglue 要\zhglue 跨} 50 \zh{个\zhglue 词} \textbar{}

\textbf{\zh{评\zhglue 价\zhglue 指\zhglue 标}}\zh{：\zhglue 困\zhglue 惑\zhglue 度} \(\mathrm{PPL}=\exp(-\frac{1}{T}\sum_t \log P(w_t\mid w_{<t}))\) ------ \zh{正\zhglue 是} M0 \zh{里\zhglue 交\zhglue 叉\zhglue 熵\zhglue 取\zhglue 指\zhglue 数\zhglue 。}\textbf{PPL \zh{越\zhglue 低\zhglue ，\zhglue 模\zhglue 型\zhglue 对\zhglue 下\zhglue 一\zhglue 个\zhglue 词\zhglue 越\zhglue 不}''\zh{惊\zhglue 讶}''\zh{。}} \zh{这\zhglue 个\zhglue 指\zhglue 标\zhglue 一\zhglue 路\zhglue 沿\zhglue 用\zhglue 到\zhglue 今\zhglue 天\zhglue （}bpb \zh{是\zhglue 它\zhglue 的\zhglue 分\zhglue 词\zhglue 器\zhglue 无\zhglue 关\zhglue 版\zhglue 本\zhglue ）\zhglue 。}

\begin{center}\rule{0.5\linewidth}{0.5pt}\end{center}

\subsection{\zh{二\zhglue 、\zhglue 词\zhglue 向\zhglue 量\zhglue ：\zhglue 从} one-hot \zh{到\zhglue 分\zhglue 布\zhglue 式\zhglue 表\zhglue 示}}\label{ux4e8cux8bcdux5411ux91cfux4ece-one-hot-ux5230ux5206ux5e03ux5f0fux8868ux793a}

\textbf{one-hot \zh{的\zhglue 问\zhglue 题}}\zh{：\zhglue 任\zhglue 意\zhglue 两\zhglue 词\zhglue 的\zhglue 内\zhglue 积\zhglue 都\zhglue 是} 0------``\zh{猫}''\zh{和}''\zh{狗}''\zh{的\zhglue 距\zhglue 离\zhglue 与}''\zh{猫}''\zh{和}''\zh{民\zhglue 主}''\zh{一\zhglue 样\zhglue 远\zhglue 。\zhglue 语\zhglue 义\zhglue 无\zhglue 从\zhglue 谈\zhglue 起\zhglue 。}

\textbf{word2vec\zh{（}skip-gram + \zh{负\zhglue 采\zhglue 样\zhglue ）}}\zh{：\zhglue 用\zhglue 一\zhglue 个}''\zh{给\zhglue 定\zhglue 中\zhglue 心\zhglue 词\zhglue 预\zhglue 测\zhglue 上\zhglue 下\zhglue 文\zhglue 词}''\zh{的\zhglue 浅\zhglue 层\zhglue 网\zhglue 络\zhglue ，} \zh{训\zhglue 练\zhglue 出\zhglue 的}\textbf{\zh{中\zhglue 间\zhglue 层\zhglue 权\zhglue 重}}\zh{就\zhglue 是\zhglue 词\zhglue 向\zhglue 量\zhglue 。\zhglue 结\zhglue 果\zhglue 是\zhglue 著\zhglue 名\zhglue 的}\textbf{\zh{向\zhglue 量\zhglue 算\zhglue 术}}\zh{：}\(\vec{king}-\vec{man}+\vec{woman}\approx\vec{queen}\)\zh{。}

\textbf{\zh{关\zhglue 键\zhglue 转\zhglue 折}}\zh{：\zhglue 人\zhglue 们\zhglue 发\zhglue 现}''\zh{预\zhglue 测\zhglue 下\zhglue 一\zhglue 个\zhglue 词}''\zh{这\zhglue 个\zhglue 任\zhglue 务\zhglue 逼\zhglue 着\zhglue 模\zhglue 型\zhglue 学\zhglue 出}\textbf{\zh{语\zhglue 义\zhglue 结\zhglue 构}}\zh{。} \zh{这\zhglue 正\zhglue 是\zhglue 后\zhglue 来} GPT \zh{路\zhglue 线\zhglue 的\zhglue 思\zhglue 想\zhglue 雏\zhglue 形\zhglue ：}\textbf{\zh{语\zhglue 言\zhglue 建\zhglue 模\zhglue 本\zhglue 身\zhglue 就\zhglue 是\zhglue 最\zhglue 好\zhglue 的\zhglue 自\zhglue 监\zhglue 督\zhglue 任\zhglue 务\zhglue 。}}

\subsection{\zh{三\zhglue 、}RNN / LSTM\zh{：\zhglue 把}''\zh{历\zhglue 史}''\zh{压\zhglue 进\zhglue 一\zhglue 个\zhglue 向\zhglue 量}}\label{ux4e09rnn-lstmux628aux5386ux53f2ux538bux8fdbux4e00ux4e2aux5411ux91cf}

\textbf{RNN}\zh{：}\(h_t = \tanh(W_h h_{t-1} + W_x x_t)\)\zh{，\zhglue 用} \(h_{t-1}\) \zh{携\zhglue 带\zhglue 历\zhglue 史\zhglue 。} \textbf{LSTM}\zh{：\zhglue 加\zhglue 门\zhglue 控\zhglue （\zhglue 遗\zhglue 忘\zhglue 门\zhglue 、\zhglue 输\zhglue 入\zhglue 门\zhglue 、\zhglue 输\zhglue 出\zhglue 门\zhglue ）\zhglue 让\zhglue 信\zhglue 息\zhglue 可\zhglue 以}''\zh{长\zhglue 期\zhglue 保\zhglue 留}''\zh{。}

\textbf{\zh{为\zhglue 什\zhglue 么\zhglue 被\zhglue 淘\zhglue 汰\zhglue （\zhglue 两\zhglue 个\zhglue 根\zhglue 因\zhglue ，\zhglue 都\zhglue 是\zhglue 工\zhglue 程\zhglue 性\zhglue 的\zhglue ）}}\zh{：}

\begin{enumerate}
\def\labelenumi{\arabic{enumi}.}
\tightlist
\item
  \textbf{\zh{梯\zhglue 度\zhglue 消\zhglue 失}/\zh{爆\zhglue 炸}}\zh{：}BPTT \zh{要\zhglue 对\zhglue 时\zhglue 间\zhglue 展\zhglue 开\zhglue 求\zhglue 导\zhglue ，\zhglue 梯\zhglue 度\zhglue 是} \(\prod_t \frac{\partial h_t}{\partial h_{t-1}}\) \zh{的\zhglue 连\zhglue 乘\zhglue 。} \zh{连\zhglue 乘\zhglue 里\zhglue 每\zhglue 个} Jacobian \zh{的\zhglue 谱\zhglue 半\zhglue 径} \textless1 \sym{→} \zh{指\zhglue 数\zhglue 衰\zhglue 减\zhglue ；}\textgreater1 \sym{→} \zh{爆\zhglue 炸\zhglue 。}\textbf{\zh{这\zhglue 是\zhglue 结\zhglue 构\zhglue 性\zhglue 问\zhglue 题\zhglue ，\zhglue 不\zhglue 是\zhglue 调\zhglue 参\zhglue 能\zhglue 解\zhglue 决\zhglue 的\zhglue 。}}
\item
  \textbf{\zh{无\zhglue 法\zhglue 并\zhglue 行}}\zh{：}\(h_t\) \zh{依\zhglue 赖} \(h_{t-1}\)\zh{，}\textbf{\zh{必\zhglue 须\zhglue 按\zhglue 时\zhglue 间\zhglue 顺\zhglue 序\zhglue 串\zhglue 行\zhglue 计\zhglue 算}}\zh{。} GPU \zh{有\zhglue 上\zhglue 万\zhglue 核\zhglue 心\zhglue 却\zhglue 只\zhglue 能\zhglue 一\zhglue 次\zhglue 算\zhglue 一\zhglue 步} ------ \zh{训\zhglue 练\zhglue 吞\zhglue 吐\zhglue 被}''\zh{时\zhglue 间}''\zh{卡\zhglue 死\zhglue 。}
\end{enumerate}

\begin{quote}
\textbf{\zh{这\zhglue 是\zhglue 本\zhglue 讲\zhglue 最\zhglue 重\zhglue 要\zhglue 的\zhglue 一\zhglue 句\zhglue 话}}\zh{：\zhglue 深\zhglue 度\zhglue 学\zhglue 习\zhglue 的\zhglue 历\zhglue 史\zhglue 不\zhglue 是}''\zh{模\zhglue 型\zhglue 更\zhglue 聪\zhglue 明}''\zh{的\zhglue 历\zhglue 史\zhglue ，} \zh{而\zhglue 是}''\textbf{\zh{让\zhglue 更\zhglue 多\zhglue 算\zhglue 力\zhglue 能\zhglue 有\zhglue 效\zhglue 地\zhglue 作\zhglue 用\zhglue 于\zhglue 更\zhglue 多\zhglue 数\zhglue 据}}''\zh{的\zhglue 历\zhglue 史\zhglue 。}Transformer \zh{赢\zhglue 在}\textbf{\zh{能\zhglue 并\zhglue 行}}\zh{。}

\subsection{\zh{四\zhglue 、}seq2seq \zh{与\zhglue 注\zhglue 意\zhglue 力\zhglue （}2014\zh{）\zhglue ：\zhglue 注\zhglue 意\zhglue 力\zhglue 的\zhglue 真\zhglue 正\zhglue 起\zhglue 源}}\label{ux56dbseq2seq-ux4e0eux6ce8ux610fux529b2014ux6ce8ux610fux529bux7684ux771fux6b63ux8d77ux6e90}

\textbf{seq2seq \zh{结\zhglue 构}}\zh{：\zhglue 一\zhglue 个} RNN \zh{把\zhglue 源\zhglue 句\zhglue 编\zhglue 码\zhglue 成\zhglue 一\zhglue 个}\textbf{\zh{固\zhglue 定\zhglue 长\zhglue 度}}\zh{向\zhglue 量\zhglue ，\zhglue 另\zhglue 一\zhglue 个} RNN \zh{从\zhglue 它\zhglue 解\zhglue 码\zhglue 出\zhglue 目\zhglue 标\zhglue 句\zhglue 。}

\textbf{\zh{瓶\zhglue 颈}}\zh{：\zhglue 整\zhglue 句\zhglue 信\zhglue 息\zhglue 被\zhglue 压\zhglue 进\zhglue 一\zhglue 个\zhglue 向\zhglue 量} ------ \zh{句\zhglue 子\zhglue 越\zhglue 长\zhglue ，\zhglue 丢\zhglue 得\zhglue 越\zhglue 多\zhglue 。}

\textbf{Bahdanau \zh{注\zhglue 意\zhglue 力\zhglue （}2014\zh{）\zhglue 的\zhglue 解\zhglue 法}}\zh{：\zhglue 解\zhglue 码\zhglue 每\zhglue 一\zhglue 步\zhglue 时\zhglue ，}\textbf{\zh{回\zhglue 头\zhglue 扫\zhglue 描\zhglue 编\zhglue 码\zhglue 器\zhglue 的\zhglue 所\zhglue 有\zhglue 隐\zhglue 状\zhglue 态}}\zh{，} \zh{算\zhglue 出}''\zh{当\zhglue 前\zhglue 该\zhglue 关\zhglue 注\zhglue 哪\zhglue 几\zhglue 个\zhglue 位\zhglue 置}''\zh{，\zhglue 加\zhglue 权\zhglue 求\zhglue 和\zhglue 作\zhglue 为\zhglue 当\zhglue 前\zhglue 步\zhglue 的\zhglue 上\zhglue 下\zhglue 文\zhglue 。} \sym{→} \zh{这\zhglue 就\zhglue 是} \textbf{QKV \zh{的\zhglue 雏\zhglue 形}}\zh{（\zhglue 解\zhglue 码\zhglue 器\zhglue 状\zhglue 态\zhglue ＝}Query\zh{，\zhglue 编\zhglue 码\zhglue 器\zhglue 隐\zhglue 状\zhglue 态\zhglue ＝}Key/Value\zh{）\zhglue 。}

\textbf{\zh{关\zhglue 键\zhglue 历\zhglue 史\zhglue 结\zhglue 论}}\zh{：}\textbf{\zh{注\zhglue 意\zhglue 力\zhglue 最\zhglue 初\zhglue 是\zhglue 给} RNN \zh{打\zhglue 补\zhglue 丁\zhglue 的}}\zh{，\zhglue 不\zhglue 是} Transformer \zh{的\zhglue 胜\zhglue 利\zhglue 品\zhglue 。} Transformer \zh{的\zhglue 贡\zhglue 献\zhglue 是\zhglue ：}\textbf{``\zh{既\zhglue 然\zhglue 注\zhglue 意\zhglue 力\zhglue 这\zhglue 么\zhglue 好\zhglue 用\zhglue ，\zhglue 那\zhglue 还\zhglue 要} RNN \zh{干\zhglue 什\zhglue 么\zhglue ？}''}------ \zh{把} RNN \zh{整\zhglue 个\zhglue 删\zhglue 掉\zhglue ，\zhglue 只\zhglue 用\zhglue 注\zhglue 意\zhglue 力} + \zh{前\zhglue 馈\zhglue 层\zhglue ，\zhglue 于\zhglue 是}\textbf{\zh{整\zhglue 段\zhglue 序\zhglue 列\zhglue 可\zhglue 以\zhglue 并\zhglue 行\zhglue 计\zhglue 算}}\zh{。}

\textbf{\zh{路\zhglue 径\zhglue 长\zhglue 度\zhglue 对\zhglue 比}}\zh{（\zhglue 理\zhglue 解}''\zh{为\zhglue 什\zhglue 么\zhglue 更\zhglue 快}''\zh{的\zhglue 关\zhglue 键\zhglue ）\zhglue ：} \textbar{} \zh{架\zhglue 构} \textbar{} \zh{任\zhglue 意\zhglue 两\zhglue 位\zhglue 置\zhglue 的\zhglue 依\zhglue 赖\zhglue 路\zhglue 径} \textbar{} \zh{并\zhglue 行\zhglue 性} \textbar{} \textbar---\textbar---\textbar---\textbar{} \textbar{} RNN \textbar{} \(O(L)\)\zh{（\zhglue 要\zhglue 穿\zhglue 过} L \zh{步\zhglue ）} \textbar{} \emo{❌} \zh{串\zhglue 行} \textbar{} \textbar{} \textbf{Transformer} \textbar{} \textbf{\(O(1)\)}\zh{（\zhglue 一\zhglue 步\zhglue 直\zhglue 达\zhglue ）} \textbar{} \emo{✅} \zh{全\zhglue 并\zhglue 行} \textbar{}

\zh{代\zhglue 价\zhglue 是\zhglue 注\zhglue 意\zhglue 力\zhglue 本\zhglue 身} \(O(L^2)\) \zh{的\zhglue 计\zhglue 算\zhglue 量}------\zh{这\zhglue 是}''\zh{用\zhglue 算\zhglue 力\zhglue 换\zhglue 并\zhglue 行}''\zh{的\zhglue 经\zhglue 典\zhglue 交\zhglue 易\zhglue ，} \zh{也\zhglue 是\zhglue 后\zhglue 来} flash attention\zh{、\zhglue 稀\zhglue 疏\zhglue 注\zhglue 意\zhglue 力\zhglue 、\zhglue 线\zhglue 性\zhglue 注\zhglue 意\zhglue 力\zhglue 等\zhglue 一\zhglue 整\zhglue 条\zhglue 研\zhglue 究\zhglue 线\zhglue 的\zhglue 起\zhglue 点\zhglue 。}

\subsection{\zh{六\zhglue 、\zhglue 能\zhglue 立\zhglue 刻\zhglue 跑\zhglue 的\zhglue 实\zhglue 验}}\label{ux516dux80fdux7acbux523bux8dd1ux7684ux5b9eux9a8c-1}

\begin{lstlisting}[language=bash]
# (*@{\zh{用}}@*) n-gram (*@{\zh{的}}@*)(*@{\zh{视}}@*)(*@{\zh{角}}@*)(*@{\zh{感}}@*)(*@{\zh{受}}@*)"(*@{\zh{上}}@*)(*@{\zh{下}}@*)(*@{\zh{文}}@*)(*@{\zh{长}}@*)(*@{\zh{度}}@*)"(*@{\zh{的}}@*)(*@{\zh{代}}@*)(*@{\zh{价}}@*)(*@{\zh{：}}@*)(*@{\zh{统}}@*)(*@{\zh{计}}@*) bigram (*@{\zh{的}}@*)(*@{\zh{稀}}@*)(*@{\zh{疏}}@*)(*@{\zh{程}}@*)(*@{\zh{度}}@*)
(*@{\zh{环}}@*)(*@{\zh{境}}@*)/.venv/bin/python - <<'PY'
import collections, re
text = open("(*@{\zh{上}}@*)(*@{\zh{游}}@*)/assignment1-basics/tests/fixtures/tinystories_sample.txt", encoding="utf-8").read().lower()
words = re.findall(r"[a-z']+", text)
uni = collections.Counter(words)

\subsection{\texorpdfstring{\zh{一\zhglue 、\zhglue 注\zhglue 意\zhglue 力\zhglue ：\zhglue 加\zhglue 权\zhglue 检\zhglue 索} + \zh{为\zhglue 什\zhglue 么\zhglue 缩\zhglue 放} \(1/\sqrt{d_k}\)}{\zh{一\zhglue 、\zhglue 注\zhglue 意\zhglue 力\zhglue ：\zhglue 加\zhglue 权\zhglue 检\zhglue 索} + \zh{为\zhglue 什\zhglue 么\zhglue 缩\zhglue 放} 1/\textbackslash sqrt\{d\_k\}}}\label{ux4e00ux6ce8ux610fux529bux52a0ux6743ux68c0ux7d22-ux4e3aux4ec0ux4e48ux7f29ux653e-1sqrtd_k}

\textbf{\zh{含\zhglue 义}}\zh{：\zhglue 每\zhglue 个\zhglue 位\zhglue 置\zhglue 发\zhglue 出\zhglue 一\zhglue 个\zhglue 查\zhglue 询} \(q\)\zh{，\zhglue 去\zhglue 和\zhglue 所\zhglue 有\zhglue 位\zhglue 置\zhglue 的\zhglue 键} \(k\) \zh{做}\textbf{\zh{内\zhglue 积}}\zh{（\zhglue 相\zhglue 似\zhglue 度\zhglue ）\zhglue ，} softmax \zh{归\zhglue 一\zhglue 化\zhglue 成\zhglue 权\zhglue 重\zhglue ，\zhglue 再\zhglue 对\zhglue 值} \(v\) \zh{做\zhglue 加\zhglue 权\zhglue 平\zhglue 均\zhglue 。}\textbf{\zh{它\zhglue 就\zhglue 是}''\zh{按\zhglue 相\zhglue 似\zhglue 度\zhglue 检\zhglue 索\zhglue 并\zhglue 汇\zhglue 总}''}\zh{。}

\textbf{\zh{为\zhglue 什\zhglue 么\zhglue 除\zhglue 以} \(\sqrt{d_k}\)}\zh{：\zhglue 设} \(q,k\) \zh{各\zhglue 分\zhglue 量\zhglue 独\zhglue 立\zhglue 、\zhglue 均\zhglue 值} 0 \zh{方\zhglue 差} 1\zh{，\zhglue 则\zhglue 内\zhglue 积} \(q\cdot k = \sum_{i=1}^{d_k} q_i k_i\) \zh{的} \zh{方\zhglue 差\zhglue 是} \(d_k\)\zh{（\zhglue 方\zhglue 差\zhglue 可\zhglue 加\zhglue ）\zhglue 。}\(d_k\) \zh{越\zhglue 大\zhglue ，\zhglue 内\zhglue 积\zhglue 的}\textbf{\zh{取\zhglue 值\zhglue 范\zhglue 围\zhglue 越\zhglue 宽}} \sym{→} softmax \zh{越\zhglue 容\zhglue 易}''\zh{饱\zhglue 和}'' \zh{（\zhglue 一\zhglue 个\zhglue 位\zhglue 置\zhglue 拿\zhglue 走\zhglue 几\zhglue 乎\zhglue 全\zhglue 部\zhglue 权\zhglue 重\zhglue ）}\sym{→} \zh{梯\zhglue 度\zhglue 趋\zhglue 近} 0 \sym{→} \zh{训\zhglue 练\zhglue 不\zhglue 动\zhglue 。} \zh{除\zhglue 以} \(\sqrt{d_k}\) \zh{把\zhglue 内\zhglue 积\zhglue 的\zhglue 方\zhglue 差\zhglue 重\zhglue 新\zhglue 拉\zhglue 回} 1 \zh{量\zhglue 级\zhglue 。}\textbf{\zh{这\zhglue 是}''\zh{训\zhglue 练\zhglue 稳\zhglue 定\zhglue 性}''\zh{的\zhglue 第\zhglue 一\zhglue 课\zhglue 。}}

\textbf{\zh{因\zhglue 果\zhglue 掩\zhglue 码}}\zh{：\zhglue 语\zhglue 言\zhglue 模\zhglue 型\zhglue 不\zhglue 能\zhglue 看\zhglue 未\zhglue 来\zhglue 。\zhglue 做\zhglue 法\zhglue 是}\textbf{\zh{在} softmax \zh{之\zhglue 前}}\zh{给\zhglue 未\zhglue 来\zhglue 位\zhglue 置\zhglue 加} \(-\infty\)\zh{（\zhglue 实\zhglue 现\zhglue 上\zhglue 常\zhglue 用} \(-10^9\) \zh{之\zhglue 类\zhglue 的\zhglue 大\zhglue 负\zhglue 数\zhglue ，} \zh{而\zhglue 不\zhglue 是} 0\zh{）}------\zh{因\zhglue 为\zhglue 在} softmax \zh{之\zhglue 后\zhglue 置\zhglue 零\zhglue 会}\textbf{\zh{改\zhglue 变\zhglue 概\zhglue 率\zhglue 归\zhglue 一\zhglue 化}}\zh{（\zhglue 等\zhglue 价\zhglue 于\zhglue 把\zhglue 已\zhglue 分\zhglue 配\zhglue 的\zhglue 概\zhglue 率\zhglue 丢\zhglue 掉\zhglue ，\zhglue 剩\zhglue 下\zhglue 的\zhglue 位\zhglue 置\zhglue 没\zhglue 有\zhglue 重\zhglue 新\zhglue 归\zhglue 一\zhglue ）\zhglue 。}

\textbf{\zh{做\zhglue 法}}\zh{：\zhglue 把} \(d\) \zh{维\zhglue 拆\zhglue 成} \(h\) \zh{份\zhglue 、\zhglue 每\zhglue 份} \(d_k = d/h\)\zh{，\zhglue 各\zhglue 自\zhglue 独\zhglue 立\zhglue 做\zhglue 注\zhglue 意\zhglue 力\zhglue ，\zhglue 再\zhglue 拼\zhglue 回\zhglue 来\zhglue 过\zhglue 一\zhglue 个\zhglue 输\zhglue 出\zhglue 投\zhglue 影} \(W_O\)\zh{。}

\textbf{\zh{关\zhglue 键\zhglue 理\zhglue 解\zhglue （\zhglue 常\zhglue 被\zhglue 误\zhglue 解\zhglue ）}}\zh{：\zhglue 多\zhglue 头}\textbf{\zh{不\zhglue 增\zhglue 加}}\zh{注\zhglue 意\zhglue 力\zhglue 的\zhglue 总\zhglue 计\zhglue 算\zhglue 量}------ \zh{单\zhglue 个\zhglue 头\zhglue 用\zhglue 满} \(d\) \zh{维\zhglue 时\zhglue ，}\(QK^\top\) \zh{是} \(L^2 d\)\zh{；}\(h\) \zh{个\zhglue 头\zhglue 各} \(d/h\) \zh{维\zhglue 合\zhglue 计\zhglue 仍\zhglue 是} \(L^2 d\)\zh{。} \zh{多\zhglue 头\zhglue 的\zhglue 收\zhglue 益\zhglue 在\zhglue 于}\textbf{\zh{归\zhglue 纳\zhglue 偏\zhglue 置}}\zh{：\zhglue 不\zhglue 同\zhglue 的\zhglue 头\zhglue 可\zhglue 以\zhglue 在\zhglue 不\zhglue 同\zhglue 子\zhglue 空\zhglue 间\zhglue 里\zhglue 学\zhglue 不\zhglue 同\zhglue 的\zhglue 关\zhglue 系} \zh{（\zhglue 有\zhglue 的\zhglue 学}''\zh{上\zhglue 一\zhglue 个\zhglue 词\zhglue 是\zhglue 谁}''\zh{，\zhglue 有\zhglue 的\zhglue 学}''\zh{括\zhglue 号\zhglue 配\zhglue 对}''\zh{，\zhglue 有\zhglue 的\zhglue 学}''\zh{句\zhglue 子\zhglue 边\zhglue 界}''\zh{）\zhglue ，\zhglue 比\zhglue 单\zhglue 一\zhglue 头\zhglue 更\zhglue 灵\zhglue 活\zhglue 。}

\textbf{\zh{变\zhglue 体\zhglue （\zhglue 现\zhglue 代\zhglue 模\zhglue 型\zhglue 常\zhglue 用\zhglue ）}}\zh{：} \textbar{} \zh{变\zhglue 体} \textbar{} \zh{做\zhglue 法} \textbar{} \zh{动\zhglue 机} \textbar{} \textbar---\textbar---\textbar---\textbar{} \textbar{} MQA \textbar{} \zh{所\zhglue 有\zhglue 头}\textbf{\zh{共\zhglue 享\zhglue 一\zhglue 份}} K/V \textbar{} KV cache \zh{减\zhglue 小} h \zh{倍\zhglue ，\zhglue 但\zhglue 质\zhglue 量\zhglue 略\zhglue 降} \textbar{} \textbar{} \textbf{GQA} \textbar{} \zh{头\zhglue 分\zhglue 组\zhglue ，\zhglue 组\zhglue 内\zhglue 共\zhglue 享} K/V\zh{（\zhglue 如} 8 \zh{组\zhglue ）} \textbar{} \zh{折\zhglue 中\zhglue ：}cache \zh{减\zhglue 小\zhglue 、\zhglue 质\zhglue 量\zhglue 几\zhglue 乎\zhglue 不\zhglue 掉} \sym{←} \zh{现\zhglue 代\zhglue 主\zhglue 流} \textbar{} \textbar{} MLA \textbar{} \zh{把} K/V \zh{压\zhglue 成\zhglue 低\zhglue 维\zhglue 潜\zhglue 变\zhglue 量\zhglue 再\zhglue 解\zhglue 压} \textbar{} \zh{更\zhglue 激\zhglue 进\zhglue ，}DeepSeek \zh{系} \textbar{}

\subsection{\zh{三\zhglue 、\zhglue 位\zhglue 置\zhglue 编\zhglue 码\zhglue ：\zhglue 从}''\zh{贴\zhglue 标\zhglue 签}''\zh{到}''\zh{转\zhglue 角\zhglue 度}''}\label{ux4e09ux4f4dux7f6eux7f16ux7801ux4eceux8d34ux6807ux7b7eux5230ux8f6cux89d2ux5ea6}

\textbf{\zh{问\zhglue 题}}\zh{：\zhglue 注\zhglue 意\zhglue 力\zhglue 的\zhglue 内\zhglue 积\zhglue 是}\textbf{\zh{置\zhglue 换\zhglue 不\zhglue 变}}\zh{的}------\zh{把} token \zh{顺\zhglue 序\zhglue 打\zhglue 乱\zhglue ，\zhglue 结\zhglue 果\zhglue 不\zhglue 变\zhglue 。\zhglue 所\zhglue 以\zhglue 必\zhglue 须\zhglue 注\zhglue 入\zhglue 位\zhglue 置\zhglue 信\zhglue 息\zhglue 。}

\textbf{\zh{三\zhglue 代\zhglue 方\zhglue 案}}\zh{：} \textbar{} \zh{方\zhglue 案} \textbar{} \zh{做\zhglue 法} \textbar{} \zh{缺\zhglue 陷} \textbar{} \textbar---\textbar---\textbar---\textbar{} \textbar{} \zh{可\zhglue 学\zhglue 习\zhglue 绝\zhglue 对\zhglue 嵌\zhglue 入} \textbar{} \zh{学\zhglue 一\zhglue 个} \(L_{\max}\times d\) \zh{的\zhglue 查\zhglue 表} \textbar{} \textbf{\zh{不\zhglue 能\zhglue 外\zhglue 推}}\zh{：\zhglue 训\zhglue 练} 2048\zh{，\zhglue 推\zhglue 理} 4096 \zh{时\zhglue 后\zhglue 一\zhglue 半\zhglue 没\zhglue 学\zhglue 过} \textbar{} \textbar{} \zh{正\zhglue 弦\zhglue 绝\zhglue 对\zhglue 编\zhglue 码\zhglue （\zhglue 原\zhglue 论\zhglue 文\zhglue ）} \textbar{} \(\sin/\cos\) \zh{固\zhglue 定\zhglue 频\zhglue 率} \textbar{} \zh{可\zhglue 外\zhglue 推\zhglue 性\zhglue 弱\zhglue ，\zhglue 实\zhglue 践\zhglue 中\zhglue 被} RoPE \zh{取\zhglue 代} \textbar{} \textbar{} \textbf{RoPE\zh{（\zhglue 旋\zhglue 转\zhglue 位\zhglue 置\zhglue 编\zhglue 码\zhglue ）}} \textbar{} \zh{把} \(q,k\) \zh{按}\textbf{\zh{二\zhglue 维\zhglue 子\zhglue 空\zhglue 间}}\zh{旋\zhglue 转\zhglue 角\zhglue 度} \(\theta\cdot m\) \textbar{} \zh{内\zhglue 积\zhglue 只\zhglue 依\zhglue 赖}\textbf{\zh{相\zhglue 对\zhglue 距\zhglue 离}} \(m-n\)\zh{；\zhglue 外\zhglue 推\zhglue 性\zhglue 好} \textbar{}

\textbf{RoPE \zh{的\zhglue 核\zhglue 心\zhglue 恒\zhglue 等\zhglue 式}}\zh{（\zhglue 这\zhglue 是\zhglue 它\zhglue 存\zhglue 在\zhglue 的\zhglue 全\zhglue 部\zhglue 理\zhglue 由\zhglue ）\zhglue ：} \[\langle R_m q,\ R_n k\rangle = \langle q,\ R_{n-m} k\rangle\] \zh{即\zhglue ：}\textbf{\zh{在\zhglue 内\zhglue 积\zhglue 里\zhglue 只\zhglue 出\zhglue 现}''\zh{位\zhglue 置\zhglue 差}''}\zh{，\zhglue 绝\zhglue 对\zhglue 位\zhglue 置\zhglue 被}''\zh{旋\zhglue 转}''\zh{吸\zhglue 收\zhglue 掉\zhglue 了\zhglue 。} \zh{推\zhglue 导\zhglue 要\zhglue 点\zhglue ：\zhglue 旋\zhglue 转\zhglue 矩\zhglue 阵\zhglue 是\zhglue 正\zhglue 交\zhglue 阵\zhglue ，}\(R_m^\top R_n = R_{n-m}\)\zh{。}

\textbf{\zh{频\zhglue 率\zhglue 分\zhglue 配}}\zh{：\zhglue 第} \(i\) \zh{对\zhglue 维\zhglue 度\zhglue 的\zhglue 角\zhglue 频\zhglue 率} \(\theta_i = 10000^{-2i/d}\)------ \zh{低\zhglue 维\zhglue （}\(i\) \zh{小\zhglue ）\zhglue 转\zhglue 得\zhglue 快\zhglue （\zhglue 管\zhglue 近\zhglue 距\zhglue 离\zhglue ）\zhglue ，\zhglue 高\zhglue 维\zhglue 转\zhglue 得\zhglue 慢\zhglue （\zhglue 管\zhglue 远\zhglue 距\zhglue 离\zhglue ）\zhglue 。} \textbf{``10000''\zh{这\zhglue 个\zhglue 底\zhglue 数\zhglue 是}''\zh{能\zhglue 覆\zhglue 盖\zhglue 多\zhglue 长\zhglue 上\zhglue 下\zhglue 文}''\zh{的\zhglue 旋\zhglue 钮}}\zh{，\zhglue 这\zhglue 就\zhglue 是\zhglue 所\zhglue 谓} YaRN / NTK \zh{插\zhglue 值\zhglue 等\zhglue 长\zhglue 上\zhglue 下\zhglue 文\zhglue 外\zhglue 推\zhglue 技\zhglue 巧\zhglue 的\zhglue 入\zhglue 口\zhglue 。}

\subsection{\zh{四\zhglue 、\zhglue 归\zhglue 一\zhglue 化\zhglue 与\zhglue 残\zhglue 差\zhglue ：\zhglue 让} 100 \zh{层\zhglue 能\zhglue 训\zhglue 起\zhglue 来\zhglue 的\zhglue 两\zhglue 件\zhglue 套}}\label{ux56dbux5f52ux4e00ux5316ux4e0eux6b8bux5deeux8ba9-100-ux5c42ux80fdux8badux8d77ux6765ux7684ux4e24ux4ef6ux5957}

\textbf{LayerNorm}\zh{：\zhglue 对\zhglue 每\zhglue 个} token \zh{的} \(d\) \zh{维\zhglue 向\zhglue 量\zhglue 做} \((x-\mu)/\sqrt{\sigma^2+\epsilon}\)\zh{，\zhglue 再\zhglue 缩\zhglue 放\zhglue 和\zhglue 平\zhglue 移\zhglue 。} \textbf{RMSNorm}\zh{：\zhglue 只\zhglue 做} \(x/\sqrt{\mathrm{mean}(x^2)+\epsilon}\)\zh{，\zhglue 再\zhglue 乘\zhglue 一\zhglue 个\zhglue 可\zhglue 学\zhglue 缩\zhglue 放} \(g\)\zh{。} - \zh{省\zhglue 掉\zhglue 了}\textbf{\zh{减\zhglue 均\zhglue 值}}\zh{与}\textbf{\zh{平\zhglue 移}}\zh{：\zhglue 更\zhglue 便\zhglue 宜\zhglue （\zhglue 少\zhglue 一\zhglue 次\zhglue 归\zhglue 约\zhglue 、\zhglue 少\zhglue 一\zhglue 组\zhglue 参\zhglue 数\zhglue ）} - \zh{效\zhglue 果\zhglue 相\zhglue 当\zhglue ：\zhglue 真\zhglue 正\zhglue 起\zhglue 作\zhglue 用\zhglue 的\zhglue 是}\textbf{\zh{尺\zhglue 度\zhglue 不\zhglue 变\zhglue 性}}\zh{（\zhglue 分\zhglue 母\zhglue 归\zhglue 一\zhglue 化\zhglue ）\zhglue ，\zhglue 而\zhglue 中\zhglue 心\zhglue 化\zhglue 贡\zhglue 献\zhglue 有\zhglue 限} - \zh{现\zhglue 代\zhglue 模\zhglue 型\zhglue （}LLaMA/Qwen/GLM \zh{系\zhglue ）\zhglue 默\zhglue 认} RMSNorm

\textbf{\zh{残\zhglue 差\zhglue 连\zhglue 接}}\zh{：}\(x \leftarrow x + f(\mathrm{norm}(x))\)\zh{（}pre-norm\zh{）\zhglue 。} \textbf{\zh{它\zhglue 防\zhglue 的\zhglue 是\zhglue 梯\zhglue 度\zhglue 消\zhglue 失}}\zh{：\zhglue 反\zhglue 向\zhglue 传\zhglue 播\zhglue 时\zhglue ，\zhglue 加\zhglue 法\zhglue 的} Jacobian \zh{是\zhglue 单\zhglue 位\zhglue 阵\zhglue ，} \zh{于\zhglue 是\zhglue 梯\zhglue 度\zhglue 有\zhglue 一\zhglue 条}\textbf{\zh{恒\zhglue 等\zhglue 通\zhglue 路}}\zh{可\zhglue 以\zhglue 直\zhglue 达\zhglue 浅\zhglue 层\zhglue 。\zhglue 没\zhglue 有\zhglue 它\zhglue ，\zhglue 几\zhglue 十\zhglue 层\zhglue 的\zhglue 网\zhglue 络\zhglue 基\zhglue 本\zhglue 训\zhglue 不\zhglue 动\zhglue 。}

\textbf{pre-norm vs post-norm}\zh{（\zhglue 原\zhglue 论\zhglue 文\zhglue 是} post-norm\zh{）\zhglue ：} \textbar{} \textbar{} \zh{形\zhglue 式} \textbar{} \zh{性\zhglue 质} \textbar{} \textbar---\textbar---\textbar---\textbar{} \textbar{} post-norm \textbar{} \(\mathrm{norm}(x + f(x))\) \textbar{} \zh{原\zhglue 版\zhglue ；\zhglue 深\zhglue 了\zhglue 需\zhglue 要\zhglue 小\zhglue 心} warmup\zh{，\zhglue 容\zhglue 易\zhglue 发\zhglue 散} \textbar{} \textbar{} \textbf{pre-norm} \textbar{} \(x + f(\mathrm{norm}(x))\) \textbar{} \zh{训\zhglue 练\zhglue 稳\zhglue ，\zhglue 是\zhglue 现\zhglue 代\zhglue 默\zhglue 认\zhglue ；\zhglue 代\zhglue 价\zhglue 是}''\zh{深\zhglue 度}''\zh{的\zhglue 表\zhglue 示\zhglue 能\zhglue 力\zhglue 稍\zhglue 弱} \textbar{}

\begin{quote}

\subsection{\texorpdfstring{\zh{五\zhglue 、}FFN\zh{、\zhglue 参\zhglue 数\zhglue 量\zhglue 与} KV cache\zh{（}\textbf{\zh{把\zhglue 结\zhglue 构\zhglue 换\zhglue 算\zhglue 成\zhglue 资\zhglue 源}}\zh{）}}{\zh{五\zhglue 、}FFN\zh{、\zhglue 参\zhglue 数\zhglue 量\zhglue 与} KV cache\zh{（\zhglue 把\zhglue 结\zhglue 构\zhglue 换\zhglue 算\zhglue 成\zhglue 资\zhglue 源\zhglue ）}}}\label{ux4e94ffnux53c2ux6570ux91cfux4e0e-kv-cacheux628aux7ed3ux6784ux6362ux7b97ux6210ux8d44ux6e90}

\textbf{FFN \zh{的\zhglue 演\zhglue 化}}\zh{：\zhglue 两\zhglue 层\zhglue 线\zhglue 性} + \zh{激\zhglue 活\zhglue 。\zhglue 原\zhglue 版} \(d_{ff} = 4d\) \zh{配} ReLU\zh{。} \textbf{SwiGLU} \zh{用\zhglue 三\zhglue 个\zhglue 矩\zhglue 阵\zhglue ：}\(\mathrm{Swish}(xW_1)\odot(xW_3)\) \zh{再\zhglue 过} \(W_2\)\zh{；} \zh{为\zhglue 保\zhglue 持\zhglue 参\zhglue 数\zhglue 量\zhglue 不\zhglue 变\zhglue ，\zhglue 取} \(d_{ff} \approx \tfrac{8}{3}d\)\zh{（}3 \zh{个\zhglue 矩\zhglue 阵} \sym{×} 8/3 \sym{≈} 4 \zh{个\zhglue 矩\zhglue 阵} \sym{×} d \zh{的\zhglue 等\zhglue 价\zhglue 参\zhglue 数\zhglue 量\zhglue ）\zhglue 。}

\textbf{\zh{单\zhglue 层\zhglue 参\zhglue 数\zhglue 量}}\zh{（}\(d\) \zh{宽\zhglue ，\zhglue 标\zhglue 准} MHA\zh{）\zhglue ：} - \zh{注\zhglue 意\zhglue 力\zhglue ：}\(W_Q,W_K,W_V,W_O\) \zh{各} \(d\times d\) \sym{→} \(4d^2\) - MLP\zh{（}SwiGLU\zh{，}\(d_{ff}=\tfrac{8}{3}d\)\zh{）\zhglue ：}\(3\cdot d\cdot \tfrac{8}{3}d = 8d^2\) - \zh{两\zhglue 处} RMSNorm \zh{缩\zhglue 放\zhglue ：}\(2d\)\zh{（\zhglue 可\zhglue 忽\zhglue 略\zhglue ）} - \textbf{\zh{合\zhglue 计} \sym{≈} \(12d^2\) \zh{每\zhglue 层}} \sym{→} \zh{全\zhglue 模\zhglue 型} \sym{≈} \(12\,L\,d^2 + Vd\)\zh{（\zhglue 权\zhglue 重\zhglue 共\zhglue 享\zhglue 时\zhglue ）}

\textbf{\zh{验\zhglue 算\zhglue 习\zhglue 惯}}\zh{：\zhglue 拿\zhglue 到\zhglue 任\zhglue 何\zhglue 模\zhglue 型\zhglue 的\zhglue 配\zhglue 置\zhglue （\zhglue 如} \passthrough{\lstinline!d=768, L=12, V=50257!}\zh{）\zhglue ，} \zh{先\zhglue 心\zhglue 算} \(12\times12\times768^2 \approx 85\text{M}\)\zh{，\zhglue 加\zhglue 嵌\zhglue 入} \(50257\times768\approx39\text{M}\)\zh{（\zhglue 共\zhglue 享\zhglue 则\zhglue 算} 1 \zh{份\zhglue ）} \sym{≈} 124M ------ \textbf{\zh{与} GPT-2 \zh{的} 124M \zh{吻\zhglue 合}}\zh{。\zhglue 这\zhglue 种}''\zh{能\zhglue 不\zhglue 能\zhglue 心\zhglue 算}''\zh{是} M3 \zh{的\zhglue 验\zhglue 收\zhglue 标\zhglue 志\zhglue 之\zhglue 一\zhglue 。}

\textbf{KV cache \zh{显\zhglue 存}}\zh{（\zhglue 推\zhglue 理\zhglue ，\zhglue 重\zhglue 点\zhglue ）\zhglue ：} \[\text{bytes} = 2 \times L_{\text{layers}} \times L_{\text{seq}} \times n_{kv} \times d_k \times \text{bytes/elem}\] \zh{（\zhglue 乘} 2 \zh{是\zhglue 因\zhglue 为} K \zh{和} V\zh{）\zhglue 。\zhglue 用} GQA \zh{后} \(n_{kv}\) \zh{从} \(h\) \zh{降\zhglue 到\zhglue 组\zhglue 数\zhglue ，} \textbf{cache \zh{直\zhglue 接\zhglue 按\zhglue 比\zhglue 例\zhglue 缩\zhglue 小}}------\zh{这\zhglue 就\zhglue 是} GQA \zh{流\zhglue 行\zhglue 的\zhglue 真\zhglue 正\zhglue 原\zhglue 因\zhglue 。} \zh{课\zhglue 题}05 \zh{会\zhglue 实\zhglue 测\zhglue 这\zhglue 个\zhglue 数\zhglue 字\zhglue 。}

\textbf{\zh{要\zhglue 回\zhglue 答\zhglue 的\zhglue 问\zhglue 题}}\zh{：\zhglue 为\zhglue 什\zhglue 么\zhglue 生\zhglue 成\zhglue 必\zhglue 须\zhglue 逐} token\zh{？}KV cache \zh{省\zhglue 了\zhglue 什\zhglue 么\zhglue 、\zhglue 代\zhglue 价\zhglue 是\zhglue 什\zhglue 么\zhglue ？\zhglue 量\zhglue 化\zhglue 掉\zhglue 多\zhglue 少\zhglue 点\zhglue ？\zhglue 吞\zhglue 吐\zhglue 与\zhglue 延\zhglue 迟\zhglue 的\zhglue 取\zhglue 舍\zhglue ？}

\subsection{M6 \sym{·} \zh{后\zhglue 训\zhglue 练\zhglue ：\zhglue 从}''\zh{会\zhglue 说\zhglue 话}''\zh{到}''\zh{会\zhglue 做\zhglue 事}''\zh{（}W10--W12\zh{）}}\label{m6-ux540eux8badux7ec3ux4eceux4f1aux8bf4ux8bddux5230ux4f1aux505aux4e8bw10w12}

\textbf{\zh{要\zhglue 回\zhglue 答\zhglue 的\zhglue 问\zhglue 题}}\zh{：}SFT / DPO / RLVR \zh{各\zhglue 补\zhglue 什\zhglue 么\zhglue 能\zhglue 力\zhglue ？\zhglue 为\zhglue 什\zhglue 么} RLVR \zh{提\zhglue 数\zhglue 学}/\zh{代\zhglue 码\zhglue 却\zhglue 可\zhglue 能\zhglue 伤\zhglue 通\zhglue 用\zhglue 能\zhglue 力\zhglue ？}reward hacking \zh{长\zhglue 什\zhglue 么\zhglue 样\zhglue ？}

\textbf{\zh{关\zhglue 键\zhglue 概\zhglue 念}}\zh{：\zhglue 指\zhglue 令\zhglue 微\zhglue 调\zhglue 与} chat template \textbar{} \textbf{\zh{损\zhglue 失\zhglue 掩\zhglue 码\zhglue （\zhglue 只\zhglue 算} answer \zh{部\zhglue 分\zhglue ）}} \textbar{} \zh{奖\zhglue 励\zhglue 模\zhglue 型} \textbar{} PPO \zh{的\zhglue 四\zhglue 个\zhglue 模\zhglue 型} \textbar{} DPO \zh{的\zhglue 隐\zhglue 式\zhglue 奖\zhglue 励\zhglue （\zhglue 推\zhglue 导\zhglue ：\zhglue 为\zhglue 什\zhglue 么\zhglue 可\zhglue 以\zhglue 不\zhglue 要} reward model\zh{）}\textbar{} \textbf{GRPO \zh{的\zhglue 组\zhglue 内\zhglue 归\zhglue 一\zhglue 化\zhglue 优\zhglue 势}} \textbar{} KL \zh{正\zhglue 则} \textbar{} \zh{拒\zhglue 绝\zhglue 采\zhglue 样} \textbar{} \zh{蒸\zhglue 馏\zhglue 与\zhglue 合\zhglue 成\zhglue 数\zhglue 据} \textbar{} \zh{过\zhglue 度\zhglue 优\zhglue 化\zhglue （}over-optimization\zh{）}

\textbf{\zh{必\zhglue 读}}\zh{：}CS336 A5\zh{（\zhglue 含} \passthrough{\lstinline!tests/test\_grpo.py!}\zh{、}GSPO/MaxRL\zh{）}\sym{·} \textbf{rasbt\zh{《}Reasoning from Scratch\zh{》}ch3--ch8} \sym{·} \textbf{Nathan Lambert\zh{《}RLHF Book\zh{》}}\zh{（\zhglue 免\zhglue 费\zhglue 在\zhglue 线\zhglue ，}17 \zh{章\zhglue ）}\sym{·} \passthrough{\lstinline!minimind/trainer/train\_grpo.py!} \sym{·} \passthrough{\lstinline!nano-aha-moment!}\zh{（\zhglue 单\zhglue 文\zhglue 件\zhglue ）}

\textbf{\zh{实\zhglue 验}}\zh{：\zhglue 同\zhglue 基\zhglue 座\zhglue 做} SFT / DPO / GRPO \zh{三\zhglue 变\zhglue 体} \sym{→} \zh{同\zhglue 一\zhglue 评\zhglue 测\zhglue 口\zhglue 径\zhglue 对\zhglue 照} \sym{→} \zh{手\zhglue 写} GRPO \zh{损\zhglue 失\zhglue 并\zhglue 逐\zhglue 项\zhglue 注\zhglue 释} \sym{→} \textbf{\zh{制\zhglue 造\zhglue 一\zhglue 次} reward hacking \zh{并\zhglue 记\zhglue 录}}

\textbf{\zh{坑}}\zh{：\zhglue 以\zhglue 为} DPO''\zh{更\zhglue 简\zhglue 单\zhglue 所\zhglue 以\zhglue 更\zhglue 好}''\textbar{} \zh{以\zhglue 为} RL \zh{一\zhglue 定\zhglue 比} SFT \zh{强} \textbar{} \textbf{\zh{把\zhglue 训\zhglue 练} reward \zh{上\zhglue 升\zhglue 当\zhglue 能\zhglue 力\zhglue 提\zhglue 升}}\zh{（\zhglue 不\zhglue 做} held-out \zh{评\zhglue 测\zhglue 就\zhglue 是\zhglue 自\zhglue 欺\zhglue ）}\textbar{} KL \zh{系\zhglue 数\zhglue 设\zhglue 错\zhglue 导\zhglue 致\zhglue 模\zhglue 型}''\zh{复\zhglue 读\zhglue 训\zhglue 练\zhglue 集\zhglue 答\zhglue 案}''

\subsection{M7 \sym{·} \zh{评\zhglue 测\zhglue ：\zhglue 把}''\zh{感\zhglue 觉\zhglue 变\zhglue 好\zhglue 了}''\zh{变\zhglue 成}''\zh{确\zhglue 实\zhglue 变\zhglue 好\zhglue 了}''\zh{（}W9--W12\zh{）}}\label{m7-ux8bc4ux6d4bux628aux611fux89c9ux53d8ux597dux4e86ux53d8ux6210ux786eux5b9eux53d8ux597dux4e86w9w12}

\textbf{\zh{要\zhglue 回\zhglue 答\zhglue 的\zhglue 问\zhglue 题}}\zh{：}benchmark \zh{分\zhglue 数\zhglue 可\zhglue 信\zhglue 吗\zhglue ？\zhglue 污\zhglue 染\zhglue 怎\zhglue 么\zhglue 判\zhglue 断\zhglue ？\zhglue 为\zhglue 什\zhglue 么\zhglue 必\zhglue 须\zhglue 固\zhglue 定\zhglue 口\zhglue 径\zhglue ？\zhglue 方\zhglue 差\zhglue 有\zhglue 多\zhglue 大\zhglue ？}

\textbf{\zh{关\zhglue 键\zhglue 概\zhglue 念}}\zh{：}bpb / PPL / CORE \textbar{} \zh{下\zhglue 游\zhglue 基\zhglue 准\zhglue （}GSM8K / MATH-500 / MMLU / HumanEval\zh{）}\textbar{} pass@k \textbar{} \zh{评\zhglue 测\zhglue 污\zhglue 染} \textbar{} few-shot \zh{与} prompt \zh{敏\zhglue 感\zhglue 性} \textbar{} \textbf{\zh{方\zhglue 差\zhglue 与\zhglue 误\zhglue 差\zhglue 棒}} \textbar{} LLM-as-judge \zh{的\zhglue 偏\zhglue 置} \textbar{} \zh{可\zhglue 验\zhglue 证\zhglue 奖\zhglue 励} vs \zh{学\zhglue 习\zhglue 型\zhglue 奖\zhglue 励}

\textbf{\zh{必\zhglue 读}}\zh{：}\textbf{rasbt ch3\zh{（\zhglue 先\zhglue 建\zhglue 评\zhglue 测\zhglue 再\zhglue 上\zhglue 技\zhglue 术}------\zh{全\zhglue 书\zhglue 教\zhglue 学\zhglue 法\zhglue 基\zhglue 石\zhglue ）}} \sym{·} \passthrough{\lstinline!nanochat/core\_eval.py!}\zh{（\zhglue 单\zhglue 文\zhglue 件} CORE\zh{）}\sym{·} CS336 lecture 12 \sym{·} Berkeley Agentic AI MOOC \zh{的} ``Agent Evaluation'' \zh{讲}

\textbf{\zh{实\zhglue 验}}\zh{：\zhglue 写\zhglue 一\zhglue 个\zhglue 数\zhglue 学\zhglue 题\zhglue 判\zhglue 定\zhglue 器\zhglue （\zhglue 解\zhglue 析} + \zh{数\zhglue 值\zhglue 比\zhglue 对} + \zh{容\zhglue 错\zhglue ）}\sym{→} \zh{测\zhglue 它\zhglue 在} 200 \zh{题\zhglue 上\zhglue 的\zhglue 准\zhglue 确\zhglue 率\zhglue 与}\textbf{\zh{假\zhglue 阳\zhglue 性}} \sym{→} \zh{给\zhglue 两\zhglue 个\zhglue 模\zhglue 型\zhglue 跑\zhglue 同\zhglue 一\zhglue 套\zhglue 评\zhglue 测\zhglue 并\zhglue 给\zhglue 误\zhglue 差\zhglue 棒}

\subsection{M8 \sym{·} \zh{智\zhglue 能\zhglue 体\zhglue ：\zhglue 工\zhglue 具\zhglue 、\zhglue 记\zhglue 忆\zhglue 、}harness\zh{、\zhglue 长\zhglue 程\zhglue 任\zhglue 务\zhglue （}W13--W14\zh{）}}\label{m8-ux667aux80fdux4f53ux5de5ux5177ux8bb0ux5fc6harnessux957fux7a0bux4efbux52a1w13w14}

\textbf{\zh{要\zhglue 回\zhglue 答\zhglue 的\zhglue 问\zhglue 题}}\zh{：\zhglue 从}''\zh{回\zhglue 答\zhglue 问\zhglue 题}''\zh{到}''\zh{完\zhglue 成\zhglue 任\zhglue 务}''\zh{缺\zhglue 什\zhglue 么\zhglue ？\zhglue 为\zhglue 什\zhglue 么} harness \zh{比\zhglue 模\zhglue 型\zhglue 本\zhglue 身\zhglue 更\zhglue 决\zhglue 定\zhglue 成\zhglue 败\zhglue ？}self-judge \zh{何\zhglue 时\zhglue 有\zhglue 效\zhglue 、\zhglue 何\zhglue 时\zhglue 自\zhglue 欺\zhglue ？}

\textbf{\zh{关\zhglue 键\zhglue 概\zhglue 念}}\zh{：\zhglue 工\zhglue 具}/\zh{函\zhglue 数\zhglue 调\zhglue 用} \textbar{} ReAct \zh{循\zhglue 环\zhglue （}Thought-Action-Observation\zh{）}\textbar{} \textbf{context engineering}\zh{（\zhglue 李\zhglue 宏\zhglue 毅\zhglue 与} Karpathy \zh{都\zhglue 专\zhglue 讲\zhglue ）}\textbar{} \zh{记\zhglue 忆\zhglue 与\zhglue 压\zhglue 缩} \textbar{} harness \zh{设\zhglue 计\zhglue （\zhglue 沙\zhglue 箱\zhglue 、\zhglue 权\zhglue 限\zhglue 、\zhglue 反\zhglue 馈\zhglue 信\zhglue 号\zhglue ）}\textbar{} \zh{长\zhglue 程\zhglue 任\zhglue 务\zhglue 的\zhglue 误\zhglue 差\zhglue 累\zhglue 积} \textbar{} \zh{多\zhglue 智\zhglue 能\zhglue 体} \textbar{} self-judge / self-refine \zh{的\zhglue 边\zhglue 界} \textbar{} \zh{可\zhglue 验\zhglue 证\zhglue 环\zhglue 境}

\textbf{\zh{必\zhglue 读}}\zh{：\zhglue 李\zhglue 宏\zhglue 毅\zhglue 《}Context Engineering\zh{》\zhglue 专\zhglue 讲} \sym{·} Berkeley LLM Agents / Agentic AI MOOC\zh{（\zhglue 含} ``Post-Training Verifiable Agents''\zh{）}\sym{·} HF Agents Course\zh{（\zhglue 三\zhglue 框\zhglue 架} + \zh{公\zhglue 开\zhglue 排\zhglue 行\zhglue 榜\zhglue ）}\sym{·} rasbt ch5\zh{（}self-refinement\zh{）}\sym{·} \passthrough{\lstinline!PrimeIntellect-ai/verifiers!} \sym{·} terminal-bench

\textbf{\zh{实\zhglue 验}}\zh{：\zhglue 手\zhglue 写\zhglue 最\zhglue 小} ReAct \zh{循\zhglue 环\zhglue （\zhglue 不\zhglue 用\zhglue 框\zhglue 架\zhglue ）}\sym{→} \zh{给\zhglue 两\zhglue 个\zhglue 工\zhglue 具\zhglue （\zhglue 计\zhglue 算\zhglue 器} + \zh{代\zhglue 码\zhglue 执\zhglue 行\zhglue ）}\sym{→} \zh{记\zhglue 录\zhglue 失\zhglue 败\zhglue 轨\zhglue 迹\zhglue 并\zhglue 分\zhglue 类} \sym{→} \zh{加} self-judge \zh{做\zhglue 消\zhglue 融}

\textbf{\zh{要\zhglue 回\zhglue 答\zhglue 的\zhglue 问\zhglue 题}}\zh{：}MoE / \zh{长\zhglue 上\zhglue 下\zhglue 文} / \zh{多\zhglue 模\zhglue 态\zhglue 改\zhglue 变\zhglue 了\zhglue 什\zhglue 么\zhglue ？\zhglue 对\zhglue 齐\zhglue 与\zhglue 安\zhglue 全\zhglue 是}''\zh{加\zhglue 一\zhglue 层}''\zh{还\zhglue 是}''\zh{改\zhglue 训\zhglue 练\zhglue 目\zhglue 标}''\zh{？}``AI \zh{训} AI''\zh{可\zhglue 行\zhglue 到\zhglue 什\zhglue 么\zhglue 程\zhglue 度\zhglue ？}

\textbf{\zh{关\zhglue 键\zhglue 概\zhglue 念}}\zh{：}MoE \zh{稀\zhglue 疏\zhglue 激\zhglue 活\zhglue （\zhglue 总\zhglue 参\zhglue 数} vs \zh{激\zhglue 活\zhglue 参\zhglue 数\zhglue ）}\textbar{} \zh{长\zhglue 上\zhglue 下\zhglue 文\zhglue 外\zhglue 推} \textbar{} \zh{多\zhglue 模\zhglue 态\zhglue 对\zhglue 齐} \textbar{} \zh{可\zhglue 解\zhglue 释\zhglue 性} \textbar{} \zh{红\zhglue 队\zhglue 与\zhglue 越\zhglue 狱} \textbar{} \zh{幻\zhglue 觉\zhglue 的\zhglue 成\zhglue 因} \textbar{} \zh{合\zhglue 成\zhglue 数\zhglue 据\zhglue 闭\zhglue 环} \textbar{} self-play \textbar{} \zh{灾\zhglue 难\zhglue 性\zhglue 遗\zhglue 忘} \textbar{} \zh{模\zhglue 型\zhglue 合\zhglue 并}

\section{\zh{对\zhglue 标\zhglue 教\zhglue 学\zhglue 资\zhglue 源\zhglue 清\zhglue 单\zhglue （}2026-09-17 \zh{核\zhglue 实\zhglue ）}}\label{ux5bf9ux6807ux6559ux5b66ux8d44ux6e90ux6e05ux53552026-09-17-ux6838ux5b9e}

\subsection{\zh{一\zhglue 、\zhglue 研\zhglue 究\zhglue 背\zhglue 景}}\label{ux4e00ux7814ux7a76ux80ccux666f}

2017 \zh{年\zhglue 《}Attention Is All You Need\zh{》\zhglue 删\zhglue 掉} RNN\zh{，\zhglue 用\zhglue 自\zhglue 注\zhglue 意\zhglue 力} + \zh{前\zhglue 馈\zhglue 层\zhglue 实\zhglue 现} \textbf{O(1) \zh{依\zhglue 赖\zhglue 路\zhglue 径} + \zh{全\zhglue 并\zhglue 行}}\zh{。} \zh{此\zhglue 后\zhglue 所\zhglue 有\zhglue 主\zhglue 流\zhglue 大\zhglue 模\zhglue 型\zhglue 都\zhglue 是\zhglue 它\zhglue 的\zhglue 变\zhglue 体\zhglue ，\zhglue 但}\textbf{\zh{细\zhglue 节\zhglue 已\zhglue 经\zhglue 换\zhglue 了\zhglue 一\zhglue 轮}}\zh{：\zhglue 正\zhglue 弦\zhglue 位\zhglue 置\zhglue 编\zhglue 码} \sym{→} RoPE\zh{；}LayerNorm \sym{→} RMSNorm\zh{；} post-norm \sym{→} pre-norm\zh{；}ReLU MLP \sym{→} SwiGLU\zh{；}MHA \sym{→} GQA\zh{。}\textbf{\zh{本\zhglue 课\zhglue 题\zhglue 要\zhglue 求\zhglue 你\zhglue 把\zhglue 现\zhglue 代\zhglue 版\zhglue 写\zhglue 出\zhglue 来\zhglue ，\zhglue 而\zhglue 不\zhglue 是\zhglue 复\zhglue 刻} 2017 \zh{版\zhglue 。}}
